Read /home/curtis/code/apposed/jgo/src/jgo/maven/_version.py 
1    """
2   Maven version parsing, comparison, and range utilities.
3   
4   This module provides three levels of version handling:
5   
6   1. SemVer Comparison (via semver library)
7      - is_semver_1x() - Check if version string is valid SemVer 1.x
8      - compare_semver() - Compare two SemVer strings
9   
10  2. Maven Version Comparison (non-SemVer)
11     - MavenVersion class - Parsed version with comparison operators
12     - compare_versions() - Compare two version strings
13  
14  3. Maven Version Ranges
15     - VersionRange class - Parsed range with bounds
16     - parse_version_range() - Parse "[1.0,2.0)" style ranges
17     - version_in_range() - Check if version satisfies range
18  
19  Reference: https://maven.apache.org/pom.html#version-order-specification
20  
21  See also the more general Java version handling in jgo.util.java.
22  """
23  
24  from __future__ import annotations
25  
26  import re
27  from functools import total_ordering
28  from typing import NamedTuple
29  
30  import semver
31  
32  # ============================================================
33  # SECTION 1: SEMVER COMPARISON
34  #
35  # Maven uses SemVer 1.x rules when both versions match the
36  # Semantic Versioning grammar with lowercase letters only.
37  # ============================================================
38  
39  # Regex for SemVer 1.x: no build metadata (+), lowercase only
40  _SEMVER_1X_PATTERN = re.compile(
41      r"^(0|[1-9]\d*)\.(0|[1-9]\d*)\.(0|[1-9]\d*)"
42      r"(?:-((?:0|[1-9]\d*|[a-z\d-]*[a-z-][a-z\d-]*)"
43      r"(?:\.(?:0|[1-9]\d*|[a-z\d-]*[a-z-][a-z\d-]*))*))?$"
44  )
45  
46  
47  def is_semver_1x(version: str) -> bool:
48      """
49      Check if version string is valid Semantic Versioning 1.x.
50  
51      SemVer 1.x differences from 2.x:
52      - No build metadata (+ suffix)
53      - Prerelease identifiers are dot-separated alphanumerics
54  
55      Maven uses SemVer 1.x rules when both versions match this grammar
56      AND use only lowercase letters.
57  
58      Args:
59          version: Version string to check
60  
61      Returns:
62          True if version is valid SemVer 1.x with lowercase letters only
63      """
64      version = version.strip()
65      if not version:
66          return False
67  
68      # SemVer 2.x allows build metadata with +, but 1.x does not
69      if "+" in version:
70          return False
71  
72      # Maven requires lowercase for SemVer path
73      if version != version.lower():
74          return False
75  
76      # Check if it matches SemVer grammar
77      return _SEMVER_1X_PATTERN.match(version) is not None
78  
79  
80  def compare_semver(v1: str, v2: str) -> int:
81      """
82      Compare two SemVer strings.
83  
84      Args:
85          v1: First version string
86          v2: Second version string
87  
88      Returns:
89          -1 if v1 < v2
90           0 if v1 == v2
91           1 if v1 > v2
92  
93      Raises:
94          ValueError: If either version is not valid SemVer
95      """
96      return semver.Version.parse(v1).compare(semver.Version.parse(v2))
97  
98  
99  # ============================================================
100 # SECTION 2: MAVEN VERSION COMPARISON (NON-SEMVER)
101 #
102 # Implements the Maven version ordering specification from:
103 # https://maven.apache.org/pom.html#version-order-specification
104 # ============================================================
105 
106 
107 class Token(NamedTuple):
108     """
109     A single version token.
110 
111     Attributes:
112         value: The token value (int for numeric, str for qualifier)
113         separator: The separator preceding this token ('.', '-', '_', or '')
114     """
115 
116     value: int | str
117     separator: str
118 
119 
120 # Qualifier ordering per Maven spec:
121 # alpha < beta < milestone < rc = cr < snapshot < "" = final = ga = release < sp
122 QUALIFIER_ORDER: dict[str, int] = {
123     "alpha": -5,
124     "a": -5,
125     "beta": -4,
126     "b": -4,
127     "milestone": -3,
128     "m": -3,
129     "rc": -2,
130     "cr": -2,
131     "snapshot": -1,
132     "": 0,
133     "final": 0,
134     "ga": 0,
135     "release": 0,
136     "sp": 1,
137 }
138 
139 # Null values that get trimmed from the end
140 _NULL_VALUES: set[str | int] = {0, "", "final", "ga", "release"}
141 
142 
143 def _tokenize(version: str) -> list[Token]:
144     """
145     Split version string into tokens per Maven spec.
146 
147     Splitting rules:
148     1. Split on delimiters: '.', '-', '_'
149     2. Split on transitions between digits and characters (treated as '-')
150     3. Replace empty tokens with 0
151 
152     Args:
153         version: Version string to tokenize
154 
155     Returns:
156         List of Token objects with values and separators
157     """
158     if not version:
159         return []
160 
161     tokens: list[Token] = []
162     current = ""
163     current_sep = ""
164     prev_is_digit: bool | None = None
165 
166     for char in version:
167         if char in ".-_":
168             # Delimiter found - emit current token
169             if current or tokens:  # Don't emit empty first token
170                 value = _parse_token_value(current)
171                 tokens.append(Token(value, current_sep))
172             current = ""
173             current_sep = char
174             prev_is_digit = None
175         elif char.isdigit():
176             # Check for transition from letter to digit
177             if prev_is_digit is False and current:
178                 value = _parse_token_value(current)
179                 tokens.append(Token(value, current_sep))
180                 current = ""
181                 current_sep = "-"  # Transitions are treated as hyphen
182             current += char
183             prev_is_digit = True
184         else:
185             # Check for transition from digit to letter
186             if prev_is_digit is True and current:
187                 value = _parse_token_value(current)
188                 tokens.append(Token(value, current_sep))
189                 current = ""
190                 current_sep = "-"  # Transitions are treated as hyphen
191             current += char
192             prev_is_digit = False
193 
194     # Emit final token
195     if current or tokens:
196         value = _parse_token_value(current)
197         tokens.append(Token(value, current_sep))
198 
199     return tokens
200 
201 
202 def _parse_token_value(s: str) -> int | str:
203     """Parse token string to int if numeric, else lowercase string."""
204     if not s:
205         return 0
206     if s.isdigit():
207         return int(s)
208     return s.lower()
209 
210 
211 def _trim_nulls(tokens: list[Token]) -> list[Token]:
212     """
213     Remove trailing null values from token list.
214 
215     Null values are: 0, "", "final", "ga", "release"
216 
217     The trimming process is repeated at each remaining hyphen from end to start.
218     For example: "1.0.0-foo.0.0" -> "1-foo"
219 
220     Args:
221         tokens: List of tokens to trim
222 
223     Returns:
224         New list with trailing nulls removed
225     """
226     if not tokens:
227         return []
228 
229     result = list(tokens)
230 
231     # Trim from the end
232     # We remove trailing nulls, and if after removal the last token is
233     # preceded by a hyphen, we might need to remove it too if it becomes null
234     changed = True
235     while changed and result:
236         changed = False
237         # Remove trailing nulls
238         while result and result[-1].value in _NULL_VALUES:
239             result.pop()
240             changed = True
241 
242     return result
243 
244 
245 def _qualifier_priority(value: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
246     """
247     Get qualifier priority for comparison.
248 
249     Returns tuple of (priority, value) where priority is:
250     - QUALIFIER_ORDER value for known qualifiers
251     - 0 for unknown qualifiers (compared alphabetically)
252     """
253     lower = value.lower() if isinstance(value, str) else ""
254     if lower in QUALIFIER_ORDER:
255         return (QUALIFIER_ORDER[lower], "")
256     # Unknown qualifiers sort after known ones, alphabetically
257     return (0, lower)
258 
259 
260 def compare_tokens(
261     a: Token | None, b: Token | None, pad_sep_a: str = "", pad_sep_b: str = ""
262 ) -> int:
263     """
264     Compare two tokens with separator context.
265 
266     Comparison rules per Maven spec:
267     - When separators are the same:
268       - Numeric tokens use integer ordering
269       - Qualifiers use QUALIFIER_ORDER, then alphabetic
270       - Numeric > Qualifier (for non-special qualifiers)
271     - When separators differ:
272       - .qualifier = -qualifier < -number < .number
273 
274     Args:
275         a: First token (or None for padding)
276         b: Second token (or None for padding)
277         pad_sep_a: Separator to use if a is None (for padding)
278         pad_sep_b: Separator to use if b is None (for padding)
279 
280     Returns:
281         -1 if a < b, 0 if a == b, 1 if a > b
282     """
283     # Handle None (padding) cases
284     if a is None and b is None:
285         return 0
286 
287     # Get effective values and separators
288     val_a = a.value if a else (0 if pad_sep_a == "." else "")
289     val_b = b.value if b else (0 if pad_sep_b == "." else "")
290     sep_a = a.separator if a else pad_sep_a
291     sep_b = b.separator if b else pad_sep_b
292 
293     # Both numeric
294     if isinstance(val_a, int) and isinstance(val_b, int):
295         if sep_a == sep_b or (sep_a in ".-_" and sep_b in ".-_"):
296             # Same separator type - direct numeric comparison
297             # But: -number < .number when separators differ
298             if sep_a != sep_b:
299                 # .number > -number > _number (treating _ as -)
300                 if sep_a == "." and sep_b in "-_":
301                     return 1
302                 if sep_a in "-_" and sep_b == ".":
303                     return -1
304             return (val_a > val_b) - (val_a < val_b)
305         return (val_a > val_b) - (val_a < val_b)
306 
307     # Both qualifiers (strings)
308     if isinstance(val_a, str) and isinstance(val_b, str):
309         prio_a = _qualifier_priority(val_a)
310         prio_b = _qualifier_priority(val_b)
311         if prio_a[0] != prio_b[0]:
312             return (prio_a[0] > prio_b[0]) - (prio_a[0] < prio_b[0])
313         # Same priority - compare alphabetically
314         return (prio_a[1] > prio_b[1]) - (prio_a[1] < prio_b[1])
315 
316     # Mixed: one numeric, one qualifier
317     # Rule: .qualifier = -qualifier < -number < .number
318     if isinstance(val_a, int):
319         # a is numeric, b is qualifier
320         # -number > qualifier, .number > qualifier
321         return 1
322     else:
323         # a is qualifier, b is numeric
324         return -1
325 
326 
327 @total_ordering
328 class MavenVersion:
329     """
330     A Maven version parsed into tokens for comparison.
331 
332     Implements the full Maven version ordering specification from:
333     https://maven.apache.org/pom.html#version-order-specification
334 
335     Examples:
336         >>> MavenVersion("1.0") < MavenVersion("1.1")
337         True
338         >>> MavenVersion("1.0-alpha") < MavenVersion("1.0")
339         True
340         >>> MavenVersion("1.0") == MavenVersion("1.0.0")
341         True
342     """
343 
344     def __init__(self, version_string: str):
345         """
346         Parse version string into comparable form.
347 
348         Args:
349             version_string: Maven version string
350         """
351         self._original = version_string
352         self._tokens = _trim_nulls(_tokenize(version_string))
353 
354     @property
355     def tokens(self) -> list[Token]:
356         """Get the parsed and trimmed tokens."""
357         return self._tokens
358 
359     def __str__(self) -> str:
360         return self._original
361 
362     def __repr__(self) -> str:
363         return f"MavenVersion({self._original!r})"
364 
365     def __eq__(self, other: object) -> bool:
366         if not isinstance(other, MavenVersion):
367             return NotImplemented
368         return self._compare(other) == 0
369 
370     def __lt__(self, other: MavenVersion) -> bool:
371         if not isinstance(other, MavenVersion):
372             return NotImplemented
373         return self._compare(other) < 0
374 
375     def __hash__(self) -> int:
376         # Hash based on trimmed tokens for consistency with __eq__
377         return hash(tuple(self._tokens))
378 
379     def _compare(self, other: MavenVersion) -> int:
380         """Compare with another MavenVersion. Returns -1, 0, or 1."""
381         tokens_a = self._tokens
382         tokens_b = other._tokens
383         max_len = max(len(tokens_a), len(tokens_b))
384 
385         for i in range(max_len):
386             tok_a = tokens_a[i] if i < len(tokens_a) else None
387             tok_b = tokens_b[i] if i < len(tokens_b) else None
388 
389             # Padding separator comes from the corresponding position in the
390             # longer version (per Maven spec)
391             pad_sep_a = tokens_b[i].separator if i < len(tokens_b) else "."
392             pad_sep_b = tokens_a[i].separator if i < len(tokens_a) else "."
393 
394             result = compare_tokens(tok_a, tok_b, pad_sep_a, pad_sep_b)
395             if result != 0:
396                 return result
397 
398         return 0
399 
400 
401 def compare_versions(v1: str, v2: str) -> int:
402     """
403     Compare two Maven version strings.
404 
405     Uses SemVer comparison if both versions are valid SemVer 1.x,
406     otherwise falls back to Maven's complex algorithm.
407 
408     Args:
409         v1: First version string
410         v2: Second version string
411 
412     Returns:
413         -1 if v1 < v2
414          0 if v1 == v2
415          1 if v1 > v2
416     """
417     v1 = v1.strip()
418     v2 = v2.strip()
419 
420     # Try SemVer fast path
421     if is_semver_1x(v1) and is_semver_1x(v2):
422         return compare_semver(v1, v2)
423 
424     # Fall back to Maven comparison
425     mv1 = MavenVersion(v1)
426     mv2 = MavenVersion(v2)
427 
428     if mv1 < mv2:
429         return -1
430     elif mv1 > mv2:
431         return 1
432     return 0
433 
434 
435 # ============================================================
436 # SECTION 3: MAVEN VERSION RANGES
437 #
438 # Parse and evaluate Maven dependency version range expressions.
439 # ============================================================
440 
441 
442 class VersionRange(NamedTuple):
443     """
444     A Maven version range specification.
445 
446     Attributes:
447         lower: Lower bound version string, or None for unbounded
448         upper: Upper bound version string, or None for unbounded
449         lower_inclusive: True for '[', False for '('
450         upper_inclusive: True for ']', False for ')'
451     """
452 
453     lower: str | None
454     upper: str | None
455     lower_inclusive: bool
456     upper_inclusive: bool
457 
458 
459 def parse_version_range(range_spec: str) -> VersionRange:
460     """
461     Parse Maven version range specification.
462 
463     Supports:
464         - Inclusive range: "[1.0,2.0]"
465         - Exclusive range: "(1.0,2.0)"
466         - Mixed: "[1.0,2.0)"
467         - Unbounded lower: "(,2.0]"
468         - Unbounded upper: "[1.0,)"
469         - Exact version: "[1.0]" (equivalent to "[1.0,1.0]")
470         - Soft requirement: "1.0" (returns unbounded range, caller handles)
471 
472     Args:
473         range_spec: Maven-style version range string
474 
475     Returns:
476         VersionRange with parsed bounds and inclusivity
477 
478     Raises:
479         ValueError: If range syntax is invalid
480     """
481     range_spec = range_spec.strip()
482     if not range_spec:
483         raise ValueError("Empty range specification")
484 
485     # Check if this is a range syntax (starts with bracket)
486     if not (range_spec.startswith("[") or range_spec.startswith("(")):
487         # Check for invalid bracket characters
488         if range_spec[0] in "{<":
489             raise ValueError(f"Invalid opening bracket: {range_spec[0]}")
490         # Soft requirement - any version acceptable, prefer this one
491         # Return unbounded range; caller should handle preference logic
492         return VersionRange(None, None, False, False)
493 
494     # Parse range syntax
495     if len(range_spec) < 3:
496         raise ValueError(f"Invalid range syntax: {range_spec}")
497 
498     # Extract opening bracket
499     if range_spec[0] == "[":
500         lower_inclusive = True
501     elif range_spec[0] == "(":
502         lower_inclusive = False
503     else:
504         raise ValueError(f"Invalid opening bracket: {range_spec[0]}")
505 
506     # Extract closing bracket
507     if range_spec[-1] == "]":
508         upper_inclusive = True
509     elif range_spec[-1] == ")":
510         upper_inclusive = False
511     else:
512         raise ValueError(f"Invalid closing bracket: {range_spec[-1]}")
513 
514     # Extract bounds (strip brackets)
515     bounds_str = range_spec[1:-1]
516 
517     # Handle exact version: "[1.0]" -> "[1.0,1.0]"
518     if "," not in bounds_str:
519         version = bounds_str.strip()
520         if not version:
521             raise ValueError(f"Empty version in range: {range_spec}")
522         return VersionRange(version, version, lower_inclusive, upper_inclusive)
523 
524     # Split on comma
525     parts = bounds_str.split(",", 1)
526     lower_str = parts[0].strip()
527     upper_str = parts[1].strip()
528 
529     # Parse bounds (empty string means unbounded)
530     lower_bound = lower_str if lower_str else None
531     upper_bound = upper_str if upper_str else None
532 
533     return VersionRange(lower_bound, upper_bound, lower_inclusive, upper_inclusive)
534 
535 
536 def version_in_range(version: str, range_spec: str | VersionRange) -> bool:
537     """
538     Check if version satisfies the given range.
539 
540     Args:
541         version: Version string to check
542         range_spec: Range specification string or VersionRange object
543 
544     Returns:
545         True if version is within range, False otherwise
546     """
547     if isinstance(range_spec, str):
548         vr = parse_version_range(range_spec)
549     else:
550         vr = range_spec
551 
552     # Unbounded range (soft requirement) matches everything
553     if vr.lower is None and vr.upper is None:
554         return True
555 
556     # Check lower bound
557     if vr.lower is not None:
558         cmp = compare_versions(version, vr.lower)
559         if vr.lower_inclusive:
560             if cmp < 0:
561                 return False
562         else:
563             if cmp <= 0:
564                 return False
565 
566     # Check upper bound
567     if vr.upper is not None:
568         cmp = compare_versions(version, vr.upper)
569         if vr.upper_inclusive:
570             if cmp > 0:
571                 return False
572         else:
573             if cmp >= 0:
574                 return False
575 
576     return True
577

"""
Maven dependency resolution for Python.

This module provides pure-Python Maven functionality including:
- Dependency resolution (transitive dependencies, scopes, exclusions)
- POM parsing and property interpolation
- Metadata querying (available versions, release/snapshot)
- Artifact downloading from Maven repositories

Class Hierarchy
---------------
Maven concepts are represented as a hierarchy of "smart" objects that
actively perform Maven operations (resolution, downloading, POM parsing).
Each level adds more specificity:

    MavenContext          — repositories + resolver configuration
      └── Project [G:A]   — groupId:artifactId pair; metadata access
            └── Component [G:A:V]  — a Project at a specific version; owns the POM
                  └── Artifact [G:A:P:C:V]  — one file (JAR, POM, sources, …)
                        └── Dependency [G:A:P:C:V:S]  — Artifact + scope/optional/exclusions

For lightweight coordinate data structures without Maven logic, see jgo.parse.

Key Classes
-----------
MavenContext
    Entry point for all Maven operations.  Holds repository configuration
    (local cache path, additional local repo storage paths, remote repo
    name→URL map) and the Resolver implementation to use.

    >>> from jgo.maven import MavenContext
    >>> ctx = MavenContext()  # defaults (Maven Central)
    >>> ctx = MavenContext(remote_repos={
    ...     "central": "https://repo1.maven.org/maven2",
    ...     "scijava": "https://maven.scijava.org/content/groups/public",
    ... })
    >>> dep = ctx.create_dependency("org.python:jython-standalone:2.7.3")
    >>> dep.artifact.resolve()  # Path to local JAR (downloads if needed)

Project
    A groupId:artifactId (G:A) pair with access to project-level Maven
    metadata (available versions, release/latest version).

    >>> project = ctx.project("org.python", "jython-standalone")
    >>> project.release     # Newest non-SNAPSHOT version string
    >>> project.latest      # Highest version (may be SNAPSHOT)
    >>> project.versions()  # List of Component objects for all known versions

Component
    A Project pinned to a specific version (G:A:V).  Resolves special tokens
    ``RELEASE`` and ``LATEST`` to concrete version strings by consulting
    project metadata.

    >>> component = project.at_version("2.7.3")
    >>> component.pom()       # POM data structure
    >>> component.artifact()  # Default JAR artifact

Artifact
    A Component with a classifier and packaging type (G:A:P:C:V).  Knows how
    to locate itself locally (checking cache and local repos) or download from
    a remote repository.

    >>> artifact = component.artifact(classifier="sources", packaging="jar")
    >>> path = artifact.resolve()  # Downloads if not cached

Dependency
    An Artifact enriched with Maven dependency metadata: scope (compile,
    runtime, test, …), optional flag, and exclusion list.

    >>> dep = ctx.create_dependency("org.python:jython-standalone:2.7.3")
    >>> dep.scope     # e.g. "compile"
    >>> dep.optional  # bool

Resolvers
---------
PythonResolver (default)
    Pure-Python resolver that parses POMs, handles BOMs/imports, and
    downloads JARs directly via HTTP — no ``mvn`` binary required.

MvnResolver
    Shells out to the ``mvn`` command line tool.  Useful when precise
    Maven behaviour is required or for complex multi-module builds.

Both implement the abstract ``Resolver`` interface, which exposes:
- ``download(artifact)`` — fetch one artifact file
- ``resolve(dependencies)`` — resolve full transitive dependency graph
- ``get_dependency_list(dependencies)`` — flat list of resolved deps
- ``get_dependency_tree(dependencies)`` — full dependency tree

Version Utilities
-----------------
MavenVersion, VersionRange, parse_version_range, version_in_range
    Maven version comparison and range support (e.g., ``[1.0,2.0)``).

compare_versions(v1, v2)
    Return negative/zero/positive (like ``cmp``) using Maven ordering rules.

POM / Metadata Classes
-----------------------
POM
    Parsed project object model with property interpolation and XPath-style
    element access.

Model
    Fully-built effective POM after parent inheritance and property
    resolution. Used internally by PythonResolver.

Metadata / MetadataXML / Metadatas
    Maven ``maven-metadata.xml`` data — available versions, release/latest
    markers, and snapshot timestamp resolution.

Example — Resolve Dependencies
-------------------------------
>>> from jgo.maven import MavenContext
>>> ctx = MavenContext()
>>> dep = ctx.create_dependency("org.python:jython-standalone:2.7.3")
>>> resolved_inputs, transitive = ctx.resolver.resolve([dep])
>>> for d in transitive:
...     print(d.groupId, d.artifactId, d.version, d.scope)

Example — Inspect Available Versions
--------------------------------------
>>> project = ctx.project("org.scijava", "scijava-common")
>>> project.release    # e.g. '2.99.3'
>>> [c.version for c in project.versions()]
"""

from ._core import (
    Artifact,
    Component,
    Dependency,
    DependencyNode,
    MavenContext,
    Project,
    Resolver,
)
from ._metadata import Metadata, Metadatas, MetadataXML
from ._model import Model
from ._pom import POM, XML
from ._profile import ProfileConstraints, detect_os_properties
from ._resolver import MvnResolver, PythonResolver

    MavenVersion,
    VersionRange,
    compare_semver,

    parse_version_range,
    version_in_range,
)

"""
Maven-native ImageJ update site tooling.

Two halves:

- ``dbxm.mvs``: the normative reference implementation of the ImageJ
  Updater's cross-site mediation algorithm (Minimum Version Selection with
  per-path exclusions), used to validate the golden test vectors that the
  Java client implementation (imagej-updater, ``net.imagej.updater.maven``)
  must also pass.

- ``dbxm.flatten``: the jgo-based "flattener" that turns a seed Maven
  project (G:A) into ``<component>``/``<managed>`` blocks for an update
  site index, per imagej-updater's doc/maven-native-update-sites.md.
"""

from .model import GA, Catalog, Component, Edge, ManagedEntry, Release
from .mvs import Resolution, Root, Warning, resolve

__all__ = [
    "GA",
    "Catalog",
    "Component",
    "Edge",
    "ManagedEntry",
    "Release",
    "Resolution",
    "Root",
    "Warning",
    "resolve",

Write /home/curtis/code/ctrueden/db-xml-maven/src/dbxm/model.py 
"""
Data model for update site component indexes.

Mirrors the index format defined in imagej-updater's
doc/maven-native-update-sites.md §3. The Java client implements the same
model in the net.imagej.updater.maven package; the two must stay in sync.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class GA:
    """
    Component identity: groupId:artifactId, plus classifier and packaging.

    Version is deliberately NOT part of identity -- it is the variable that
    mediation selects. Default classifier is "" and default packaging "jar";
    the string form omits them when default.
    """

    group: str
    artifact: str
    classifier: str = ""
    packaging: str = "jar"

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        if self.classifier or self.packaging != "jar":
            return f"{self.group}:{self.artifact}:{self.classifier}:{self.packaging}"
        return f"{self.group}:{self.artifact}"

    @staticmethod
    def parse(s: str) -> "GA":
        tokens = s.split(":")
        if len(tokens) == 2:
            return GA(tokens[0], tokens[1])
        if len(tokens) == 4:
            return GA(tokens[0], tokens[1], tokens[2], tokens[3] or "jar")
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid GA (expected g:a or g:a:c:p): {s}")


def parse_coordinate(s: str) -> tuple[GA, str]:
    """Parse "g:a:v" (or "g:a:c:p:v") into (GA, version)."""
    tokens = s.split(":")
    if len(tokens) == 3:
        return GA(tokens[0], tokens[1]), tokens[2]
    if len(tokens) == 5:
        return GA(tokens[0], tokens[1], tokens[2], tokens[3] or "jar"), tokens[4]
    raise ValueError(f"Invalid coordinate (expected g:a:v or g:a:c:p:v): {s}")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Exclusion:
    """
    A g:a exclusion pattern; either field may be the wildcard "*".
    Exclusions never carry versions, classifiers, or packaging (Maven rule).
    """

    group: str
    artifact: str

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return f"{self.group}:{self.artifact}"

    @staticmethod
    def parse(s: str) -> "Exclusion":
        tokens = s.split(":")
        if len(tokens) != 2:
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid exclusion (expected g:a, * allowed): {s}")
        return Exclusion(tokens[0], tokens[1])

    def matches(self, ga: GA) -> bool:
        return (self.group == "*" or self.group == ga.group) and (
            self.artifact == "*" or self.artifact == ga.artifact
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Edge:
    """A direct dependency edge: target identity at a concrete version."""

    ga: GA
    version: str
    exclusions: tuple[Exclusion, ...] = ()
    platform: str | None = None
    optional: bool = False


@dataclass
class Release:
    """One version of a component, with its direct dependency edges."""

    version: str
    edges: list[Edge] = field(default_factory=list)
    sha1: str | None = None
    timestamp: str | None = None
    filesize: int | None = None
    min_java: int | None = None
    description: str | None = None
    # Slim entries carry identity+sha1 for local-file recognition only;
    # they have no (reliable) edges and are not installable.
    slim: bool = False


@dataclass
class Component:
    """All known versions of one G:A, plus site-level flags."""

    ga: GA
    releases: dict[str, Release] = field(default_factory=dict)
    offered: bool = False
    current: str | None = None

    def add(self, release: Release) -> None:
        self.releases[release.version] = release


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ManagedEntry:
    """One <managed><version> entry: a BOM-pinned version with exclusions."""

    ga: GA
    version: str
    exclusions: tuple[Exclusion, ...] = ()


@dataclass
class Catalog:
    """
    The union of component facts visible to mediation: all <component>
    blocks (across all enabled sites) plus the <managed> BOM entries.
    """

    components: dict[GA, Component] = field(default_factory=dict)
    managed: dict[GA, ManagedEntry] = field(default_factory=dict)
    bom: str | None = None

    def component(self, ga: GA) -> Component:
        c = self.components.get(ga)
        if c is None:
            c = Component(ga)
            self.components[ga] = c
        return c

    def release(self, ga: GA, version: str) -> Release | None:
        c = self.components.get(ga)
        return None if c is None else c.releases.get(version)

    def merge(self, other: "Catalog") -> None:
        """Merge another site's catalog into this one (union of facts)."""
        for ga, comp in other.components.items():
            mine = self.component(ga)
            for v, rel in comp.releases.items():
                # Facts are derived from immutable POMs; a full entry wins
                # over a slim entry, otherwise first writer wins.
                existing = mine.releases.get(v)
                if existing is None or (existing.slim and not rel.slim):
                    mine.releases[v] = rel
            mine.offered = mine.offered or comp.offered
            if comp.current is not None:
                mine.current = comp.current
        for ga, entry in other.managed.items():
            self.managed.setdefault(ga, entry)
        if other.bom is not None and self.bom is None:
            self.bom = other.bom

Write /home/curtis/code/ctrueden/db-xml-maven/src/dbxm/mvs.py 
"""
Minimum Version Selection (MVS) mediation -- normative reference.

This is the cross-site composition algorithm defined in imagej-updater's
doc/maven-native-update-sites.md §4.1/§4.2. The Java client
(net.imagej.updater.maven.MVSResolver) must produce identical results;
both implementations are validated against the golden vectors in
vectors/*.xml.

Semantics summary:

- The installation is a synthetic root; user-selected components are its
  direct dependencies ("roots"), each at the site's current version or a
  user-pinned version.
- Selection per G:A(:C:P) is the highest version requested via any
  surviving (non-excluded) path. Because published edges carry concrete
  versions only, MVS and highest-wins coincide.
- Precedence: user pin > BOM (<managed>) > MVS.
- Exclusions are per-path: an edge's exclusion set applies to the entire
  subtree traversed through that edge. Root exclusions (user "never
  install") apply globally. A BOM entry's exclusions apply to the managed
  component's outgoing edges wherever it appears (Maven depMgmt-exclusion
  semantics). Exclusions never remove an explicitly selected root.
- The walk traverses each component at its *selected* version, so losing
  subgraphs are pruned (a dependency dropped between versions is never
  reached once the newer version wins).
- Fixpoint iteration: walk -> select -> repeat until stable.

Warnings (never blocking):

- held-below: a pin or BOM entry holds a component below some surviving
  requested version.
- major-misalignment: the selected version's leading numeric ("major")
  differs from some surviving request's major (SemVer-flavored heuristic).
- missing-release: the selected version has no catalog entry (closure
  violation upstream, or a slim-only component was selected).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from functools import cmp_to_key
import re

from jgo.maven import compare_versions

from .model import GA, Catalog, Exclusion

#: Iteration cap for the fixpoint loop. Real dependency graphs converge in
#: a handful of iterations; the cap guards against pathological oscillation.
MAX_ITERATIONS = 1000

HELD_BELOW = "held-below"
MAJOR_MISALIGNMENT = "major-misalignment"
MISSING_RELEASE = "missing-release"

_version_key = cmp_to_key(compare_versions)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Root:
    """A user-selected component: site current version, or a pinned one."""

    ga: GA
    version: str
    pinned: bool = False


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Warning:
    type: str
    ga: GA

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return f"{self.type}({self.ga})"


@dataclass
class Resolution:
    #: Final selection: one version per reachable G:A.
    selected: dict[GA, str]
    #: Deduplicated, deterministically ordered warnings.
    warnings: list[Warning]
    #: Surviving requests from the stable walk (per G:A), for diagnostics.
    requests: dict[GA, set[str]] = field(default_factory=dict)


def resolve(
    catalog: Catalog,
    roots: list[Root],
    root_exclusions: tuple[Exclusion, ...] = (),
) -> Resolution:
    pins = {r.ga: r.version for r in roots if r.pinned}
    selected: dict[GA, str] = {}
    requests: dict[GA, set[str]] = {}

    for _ in range(MAX_ITERATIONS):
        requests = _walk(catalog, roots, root_exclusions, selected)
        new_selected: dict[GA, str] = {}
        for ga, versions in requests.items():
            version = max(versions, key=_version_key)
            if ga in pins:
                version = pins[ga]
            elif ga in catalog.managed:
                version = catalog.managed[ga].version
            new_selected[ga] = version
        if new_selected == selected:
            break
        selected = new_selected
    else:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"MVS did not converge within {MAX_ITERATIONS} iterations"
        )

    return Resolution(selected, _warnings(catalog, selected, requests), requests)


def _walk(
    catalog: Catalog,
    roots: list[Root],
    root_exclusions: tuple[Exclusion, ...],
    selected: dict[GA, str],
) -> dict[GA, set[str]]:
    """
    One traversal from the synthetic root, honoring the current selection.

    Returns the requested versions per G:A discovered via surviving paths.
    """
    requests: dict[GA, set[str]] = {}
    # (ga, requested version, accumulated exclusion set along this path)
    stack: list[tuple[GA, str, frozenset[Exclusion]]] = []
    global_exclusions = frozenset(root_exclusions)

    # Roots are seeded unconditionally: exclusions never remove an
    # explicitly selected root.
    for root in roots:
        requests.setdefault(root.ga, set()).add(root.version)
        stack.append((root.ga, root.version, global_exclusions))

    # Visited keys include the exclusion set: the same node may be pruned
    # differently on different paths, and only surviving paths request.
    visited: set[tuple[GA, str, frozenset[Exclusion]]] = set()

    while stack:
        ga, requested, exclusions = stack.pop()
        # Traverse at the *selected* version (pruning of losing subtrees).
        version = selected.get(ga, requested)
        state = (ga, version, exclusions)
        if state in visited:
            continue
        visited.add(state)

        release = catalog.release(ga, version)
        if release is None or release.slim:
            continue  # leaf: unknown or recognition-only entry

        # BOM-managed exclusions apply to this component's outgoing edges
        # wherever it appears (Maven depMgmt-exclusion semantics).
        managed = catalog.managed.get(ga)
        node_exclusions = exclusions
        if managed is not None and managed.exclusions:
            node_exclusions = exclusions | set(managed.exclusions)

        for edge in release.edges:
            if edge.optional:
                continue  # optional deps of dependencies are not traversed
            if any(e.matches(edge.ga) for e in node_exclusions):
                continue  # pruned on this path
            requests.setdefault(edge.ga, set()).add(edge.version)
            child_exclusions = node_exclusions
            if edge.exclusions:
                child_exclusions = node_exclusions | set(edge.exclusions)
            stack.append((edge.ga, edge.version, frozenset(child_exclusions)))

    return requests


def _major(version: str) -> int | None:
    m = re.match(r"(\d+)", version)
    return int(m.group(1)) if m else None


def _warnings(
    catalog: Catalog,
    selected: dict[GA, str],
    requests: dict[GA, set[str]],
) -> list[Warning]:
    found: set[Warning] = set()
    for ga, versions in requests.items():
        chosen = selected[ga]
        highest = max(versions, key=_version_key)
        if compare_versions(chosen, highest) < 0:
            found.add(Warning(HELD_BELOW, ga))
        chosen_major = _major(chosen)
        if chosen_major is not None and any(
            (m := _major(v)) is not None and m != chosen_major for v in versions
        ):
            found.add(Warning(MAJOR_MISALIGNMENT, ga))
        if catalog.release(ga, chosen) is None:
            found.add(Warning(MISSING_RELEASE, ga))
    return sorted(found, key=lambda w: (w.type, str(w.ga)))

Write /home/curtis/code/ctrueden/db-xml-maven/src/dbxm/xmlio.py 
"""
XML reading/writing for component indexes and mediation vectors.

Index format per imagej-updater's doc/maven-native-update-sites.md §3.
Vector format wraps a <catalog> (component/managed blocks) with <roots>
and <expected> blocks; see vectors/README.md.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from xml.etree import ElementTree as ET

from .model import (
    GA,
    Catalog,
    Component,
    Edge,
    Exclusion,
    ManagedEntry,
    Release,
    parse_coordinate,
)
from .mvs import Root, Warning


def parse_component(el: ET.Element) -> Component:
    ga = GA.parse(el.get("coordinate"))
    component = Component(
        ga,
        offered=el.get("offered") == "true",
        current=el.get("current"),
    )
    for rel_el in el.findall("release"):
        release = Release(
            version=rel_el.get("version"),
            sha1=rel_el.get("sha1"),
            timestamp=rel_el.get("timestamp"),
            filesize=int(rel_el.get("filesize")) if rel_el.get("filesize") else None,
            min_java=int(rel_el.get("min-java")) if rel_el.get("min-java") else None,
            slim=rel_el.get("slim") == "true",
        )
        desc_el = rel_el.find("description")
        if desc_el is not None:
            release.description = desc_el.text
        for dep_el in rel_el.findall("dependency"):
            dep_ga, version = parse_coordinate(dep_el.get("coordinate"))
            classifier = dep_el.get("classifier")
            packaging = dep_el.get("packaging")
            if classifier or packaging:
                dep_ga = GA(
                    dep_ga.group,
                    dep_ga.artifact,
                    classifier or "",
                    packaging or "jar",
                )
            release.edges.append(
                Edge(
                    ga=dep_ga,
                    version=version,
                    exclusions=tuple(
                        Exclusion.parse(x.text.strip())
                        for x in dep_el.findall("exclude")
                    ),
                    platform=dep_el.get("platform"),
                    optional=dep_el.get("optional") == "true",
                )
            )
        component.add(release)
    return component


def parse_managed(el: ET.Element, catalog: Catalog) -> None:
    catalog.bom = el.get("bom")
    for v_el in el.findall("version"):
        ga, version = parse_coordinate(v_el.get("coordinate"))
        catalog.managed[ga] = ManagedEntry(
            ga=ga,
            version=version,
            exclusions=tuple(
                Exclusion.parse(x.text.strip()) for x in v_el.findall("exclude")
            ),
        )


def parse_catalog(el: ET.Element) -> Catalog:
    """Parse <component> and <managed> children of the given element."""
    catalog = Catalog()
    for comp_el in el.findall("component"):
        component = parse_component(comp_el)
        catalog.components[component.ga] = component
    managed_el = el.find("managed")
    if managed_el is not None:
        parse_managed(managed_el, catalog)
    return catalog


# -- Mediation vectors --


@dataclass
class Vector:
    name: str
    description: str
    catalog: Catalog
    roots: list[Root]
    root_exclusions: tuple[Exclusion, ...]
    expected_selected: dict[GA, str]
    expected_warnings: set[Warning] = field(default_factory=set)


def load_vector(path: Path | str) -> Vector:
    root_el = ET.parse(str(path)).getroot()
    if root_el.tag != "mediation-vector":
        raise ValueError(f"Not a mediation vector: {path}")

    desc_el = root_el.find("description")
    catalog = parse_catalog(root_el.find("catalog"))

    roots: list[Root] = []
    root_exclusions: list[Exclusion] = []
    roots_el = root_el.find("roots")
    for r_el in roots_el.findall("root"):
        ga, version = parse_coordinate(r_el.get("coordinate"))
        roots.append(Root(ga, version, pinned=r_el.get("pinned") == "true"))
    for x_el in roots_el.findall("exclude"):
        root_exclusions.append(Exclusion.parse(x_el.text.strip()))

    expected_el = root_el.find("expected")
    expected_selected: dict[GA, str] = {}
    for s_el in expected_el.findall("select"):
        ga, version = parse_coordinate(s_el.get("coordinate"))
        expected_selected[ga] = version
    expected_warnings = {
        Warning(w_el.get("type"), GA.parse(w_el.get("coordinate")))
        for w_el in expected_el.findall("warning")
    }

    return Vector(
        name=root_el.get("name"),
        description=(desc_el.text or "").strip() if desc_el is not None else "",
        catalog=catalog,
        roots=roots,
        root_exclusions=tuple(root_exclusions),
        expected_selected=expected_selected,
        expected_warnings=expected_warnings,
    )

Write /home/curtis/code/ctrueden/db-xml-maven/vectors/README.md 
# Golden mediation vectors

These XML files are the conformance suite for the ImageJ Updater's
cross-site mediation algorithm (MVS with per-path exclusions), as
specified in imagej-updater's `doc/maven-native-update-sites.md` §4.

Two implementations must pass every vector, including with the roots in
reversed order (results must be enable-order independent):

- `dbxm.mvs` (this repo) — the Python reference implementation.
- `net.imagej.updater.maven.MVSResolver` (imagej-updater) — the Java
  client implementation. Vectors are copied into that repo's
  `src/test/resources/net/imagej/updater/maven/vectors/`; keep in sync.

`index.txt` lists the vector filenames; both test harnesses read it.

## Format

```xml
<mediation-vector name="...">
  <description>...</description>
  <catalog>
    <component coordinate="g:a" [offered="true"] [current="V"]>
      <release version="V" [slim="true"] [min-java="N"]>
        <dependency coordinate="g:a:v" [platform="..."] [optional="true"]>
          <exclude>g:a</exclude>   <!-- '*' wildcards allowed -->
        </dependency>
      </release>
    </component>
    <managed bom="g:a:v">
      <version coordinate="g:a:v"><exclude>g:a</exclude></version>
    </managed>
  </catalog>
  <roots>
    <root coordinate="g:a:v" [pinned="true"]/>
    <exclude>g:a</exclude>   <!-- user-level "never install" -->
  </roots>
  <expected>
    <select coordinate="g:a:v"/>       <!-- exact final selection -->
    <warning type="..." coordinate="g:a"/>  <!-- exact warning set -->
  </expected>
</mediation-vector>
```

Warning types: `held-below`, `major-misalignment`, `missing-release`.

<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<mediation-vector name="cycle-tolerated">
  <description>
    Dependency cycles (rare but not impossible in published POMs) must
    terminate cleanly: both components are selected, no infinite walk.
  </description>

      <release version="1">
        <dependency coordinate="cyc:a:1"/>
      </release>

  </catalog>
  <roots>
    <root coordinate="cyc:a:1"/>
  </roots>
  <expected>
    <select coordinate="cyc:a:1"/>
    <select coordinate="cyc:b:1"/>
  </expected>
</mediation-vector>

Write /home/curtis/code/ctrueden/db-xml-maven/tests/test_vectors.py 
"""
Run every golden mediation vector through the reference implementation,
in declared root order and reversed (results must be enable-order
independent).
"""

from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from dbxm.mvs import resolve
from dbxm.xmlio import load_vector

VECTORS_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "vectors"
VECTOR_NAMES = [
    line.strip()
    for line in (VECTORS_DIR / "index.txt").read_text().splitlines()
    if line.strip()
]


def test_index_complete():
    """Every vector file on disk is listed in index.txt, and vice versa."""
    on_disk = {p.name for p in VECTORS_DIR.glob("*.xml")}
    assert on_disk == set(VECTOR_NAMES)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", VECTOR_NAMES)
@pytest.mark.parametrize("reverse_roots", [False, True])
def test_vector(name: str, reverse_roots: bool):
    vector = load_vector(VECTORS_DIR / name)
    roots = list(reversed(vector.roots)) if reverse_roots else vector.roots
    resolution = resolve(vector.catalog, roots, vector.root_exclusions)
    assert resolution.selected == vector.expected_selected, (
        f"{name}: selection mismatch.\n"
        f"  expected: { {str(k): v for k, v in sorted(vector.expected_selected.items(), key=lambda i: str(i[0]))} }\n"
        f"  actual:   { {str(k): v for k, v in sorted(resolution.selected.items(), key=lambda i: str(i[0]))} }"
    )
    assert set(resolution.warnings) == vector.expected_warnings, (
        f"{name}: warnings mismatch.\n"
        f"  expected: {sorted(str(w) for w in vector.expected_warnings)}\n"
        f"  actual:   {sorted(str(w) for w in resolution.warnings)}"
    )

# Maven version ordering corpus, ascending. A line beginning with '='
# is equal to the previous line. Both the Python reference (via jgo's
# compare_versions) and the Java client (net.imagej.updater.maven.
# MavenVersion) must order these identically. Deliberately restricted to
# the well-behaved subset where jgo's SemVer fast path and the Maven
# version-order specification agree.
0.9
1.0-alpha
1.0-alpha2
1.0-beta
1.0-milestone
1.0-rc
1.0-SNAPSHOT
1.0
=1.0.0
=1.0-ga
=1.0-final
=1.0-release
1.0-sp
1.0.1
1.1-SNAPSHOT
1.1
1.2
1.10
2.0
2.0.1
2.5.0
2.6.4

Write /home/curtis/code/ctrueden/db-xml-maven/tests/test_version_order.py 
"""Validate the shared version-ordering corpus against jgo's comparator."""

from pathlib import Path

from jgo.maven import compare_versions

CORPUS = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "vectors" / "version-order.txt"


def _load():
    groups = []  # list of equality groups, ascending
    for line in CORPUS.read_text().splitlines():
        line = line.strip()
        if not line or line.startswith("#"):
            continue
        if line.startswith("="):
            groups[-1].append(line[1:])
        else:
            groups.append([line])
    return groups


def test_corpus_ordering():
    groups = _load()
    assert len(groups) > 10
    for group in groups:
        first = group[0]
        for other in group[1:]:
            assert compare_versions(first, other) == 0, f"{first} != {other}"
    for lower, higher in zip(groups, groups[1:]):
        for a in lower:
            for b in higher:
                assert compare_versions(a, b) < 0, f"expected {a} < {b}"
                assert compare_versions(b, a) > 0, f"expected {b} > {a}"

/*
 * #%L
 * ImageJ software for multidimensional image processing and analysis.
 * %%
 * Copyright (C) 2009 - 2025 Board of Regents of the University of
 * Wisconsin-Madison, Broad Institute of MIT and Harvard, and Max Planck
 * Institute of Molecular Cell Biology and Genetics.
 * %%
 * Redistribution and use in source and binary forms, with or without
 * modification, are permitted provided that the following conditions are met:
 *
 * 1. Redistributions of source code must retain the above copyright notice,
 *    this list of conditions and the following disclaimer.
 * 2. Redistributions in binary form must reproduce the above copyright notice,
 *    this list of conditions and the following disclaimer in the documentation
 *    and/or other materials provided with the distribution.
 *
 * THIS SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED BY THE COPYRIGHT HOLDERS AND CONTRIBUTORS "AS IS"
 * AND ANY EXPRESS OR IMPLIED WARRANTIES, INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, THE
 * IMPLIED WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY AND FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE
 * ARE DISCLAIMED. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE COPYRIGHT HOLDERS OR CONTRIBUTORS BE
 * LIABLE FOR ANY DIRECT, INDIRECT, INCIDENTAL, SPECIAL, EXEMPLARY, OR
 * CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES (INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, PROCUREMENT OF
 * SUBSTITUTE GOODS OR SERVICES; LOSS OF USE, DATA, OR PROFITS; OR BUSINESS
 * INTERRUPTION) HOWEVER CAUSED AND ON ANY THEORY OF LIABILITY, WHETHER IN
 * CONTRACT, STRICT LIABILITY, OR TORT (INCLUDING NEGLIGENCE OR OTHERWISE)
 * ARISING IN ANY WAY OUT OF THE USE OF THIS SOFTWARE, EVEN IF ADVISED OF THE
 * POSSIBILITY OF SUCH DAMAGE.
 * #L%
 */

package net.imagej.updater.maven;

/**
 * Component identity: groupId:artifactId, plus classifier and packaging.
 * <p>
 * Version is deliberately <em>not</em> part of identity — it is the variable
 * that mediation selects. Default classifier is {@code ""} and default
 * packaging {@code "jar"}; the string form omits them when default. Mirrors
 * {@code dbxm.model.GA} in the db-xml-maven reference implementation.
 * </p>
 */
public final class GA implements Comparable<GA> {

    private final String group;
    private final String artifact;
    private final String classifier;
    private final String packaging;

    public GA(final String group, final String artifact) {
        this(group, artifact, "", "jar");
    }

    public GA(final String group, final String artifact,
        final String classifier, final String packaging)
    {
        this.group = group;
        this.artifact = artifact;
        this.classifier = classifier == null ? "" : classifier;
        this.packaging = packaging == null || packaging.isEmpty() ? "jar" : packaging;
    }

    public String group() { return group; }
    public String artifact() { return artifact; }
    public String classifier() { return classifier; }
    public String packaging() { return packaging; }

    /** Parses {@code "g:a"} or {@code "g:a:c:p"}. */
    public static GA parse(final String s) {
        final String[] tokens = s.split(":", -1);
        if (tokens.length == 2) return new GA(tokens[0], tokens[1]);
        if (tokens.length == 4) {
            return new GA(tokens[0], tokens[1], tokens[2], tokens[3]);
        }
        throw new IllegalArgumentException(
            "Invalid GA (expected g:a or g:a:c:p): " + s);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean equals(final Object o) {
        if (!(o instanceof GA)) return false;
        final GA other = (GA) o;
        return group.equals(other.group) && artifact.equals(other.artifact) &&
            classifier.equals(other.classifier) && packaging.equals(other.packaging);
    }

    @Override
    public int hashCode() {
        return ((group.hashCode() * 31 + artifact.hashCode()) * 31 +
            classifier.hashCode()) * 31 + packaging.hashCode();
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        if (classifier.isEmpty() && "jar".equals(packaging)) {
            return group + ":" + artifact;
        }
        return group + ":" + artifact + ":" + classifier + ":" + packaging;
    }

    @Override
    public int compareTo(final GA other) {
        return toString().compareTo(other.toString());
    }
}

 *    this list of conditions and the following disclaimer in the documentation
 *    and/or other materials provided with the distribution.
 *
 * THIS SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED BY THE COPYRIGHT HOLDERS AND CONTRIBUTORS "AS IS"
 * AND ANY EXPRESS OR IMPLIED WARRANTIES, INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, THE
 * IMPLIED WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY AND FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE
 * ARE DISCLAIMED. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE COPYRIGHT HOLDERS OR CONTRIBUTORS BE
 * LIABLE FOR ANY DIRECT, INDIRECT, INCIDENTAL, SPECIAL, EXEMPLARY, OR
 * CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES (INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, PROCUREMENT OF
 * SUBSTITUTE GOODS OR SERVICES; LOSS OF USE, DATA, OR PROFITS; OR BUSINESS
 * INTERRUPTION) HOWEVER CAUSED AND ON ANY THEORY OF LIABILITY, WHETHER IN
 * CONTRACT, STRICT LIABILITY, OR TORT (INCLUDING NEGLIGENCE OR OTHERWISE)
 * ARISING IN ANY WAY OUT OF THE USE OF THIS SOFTWARE, EVEN IF ADVISED OF THE
 * POSSIBILITY OF SUCH DAMAGE.
 * #L%
 */

package net.imagej.updater.maven;

/** A {@link GA} identity at a concrete version: {@code g:a:v}. */
public final class Coordinate {

    private final GA ga;
    private final String version;

    public Coordinate(final GA ga, final String version) {
        this.ga = ga;
        this.version = version;
    }

    public GA ga() { return ga; }
    public String version() { return version; }

    /** Parses {@code "g:a:v"} or {@code "g:a:c:p:v"}. */
    public static Coordinate parse(final String s) {
        final String[] tokens = s.split(":", -1);
        if (tokens.length == 3) {
            return new Coordinate(new GA(tokens[0], tokens[1]), tokens[2]);
        }
        if (tokens.length == 5) {
            return new Coordinate(
                new GA(tokens[0], tokens[1], tokens[2], tokens[3]), tokens[4]);
        }
        throw new IllegalArgumentException(
            "Invalid coordinate (expected g:a:v or g:a:c:p:v): " + s);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean equals(final Object o) {
        if (!(o instanceof Coordinate)) return false;
        final Coordinate other = (Coordinate) o;
        return ga.equals(other.ga) && version.equals(other.version);
    }

    @Override
    public int hashCode() {
        return ga.hashCode() * 31 + version.hashCode();
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return ga + ":" + version;
    }
}

/*
 * #%L
 * ImageJ software for multidimensional image processing and analysis.
 * %%
 * Copyright (C) 2009 - 2025 Board of Regents of the University of
 * Wisconsin-Madison, Broad Institute of MIT and Harvard, and Max Planck
 * Institute of Molecular Cell Biology and Genetics.
 * %%
 * Redistribution and use in source and binary forms, with or without
 * modification, are permitted provided that the following conditions are met:
 *
 * 1. Redistributions of source code must retain the above copyright notice,
 *    this list of conditions and the following disclaimer.
 * 2. Redistributions in binary form must reproduce the above copyright notice,
 *    this list of conditions and the following disclaimer in the documentation
 *    and/or other materials provided with the distribution.
 *
 * THIS SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED BY THE COPYRIGHT HOLDERS AND CONTRIBUTORS "AS IS"
 * AND ANY EXPRESS OR IMPLIED WARRANTIES, INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, THE
 * IMPLIED WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY AND FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE
 * ARE DISCLAIMED. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE COPYRIGHT HOLDERS OR CONTRIBUTORS BE
 * LIABLE FOR ANY DIRECT, INDIRECT, INCIDENTAL, SPECIAL, EXEMPLARY, OR
 * CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES (INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, PROCUREMENT OF
 * SUBSTITUTE GOODS OR SERVICES; LOSS OF USE, DATA, OR PROFITS; OR BUSINESS
 * INTERRUPTION) HOWEVER CAUSED AND ON ANY THEORY OF LIABILITY, WHETHER IN
 * CONTRACT, STRICT LIABILITY, OR TORT (INCLUDING NEGLIGENCE OR OTHERWISE)
 * ARISING IN ANY WAY OUT OF THE USE OF THIS SOFTWARE, EVEN IF ADVISED OF THE
 * POSSIBILITY OF SUCH DAMAGE.
 * #L%
 */

package net.imagej.updater.maven;

/**
 * A {@code g:a} exclusion pattern; either field may be the wildcard
 * {@code *}. Exclusions never carry versions, classifiers, or packaging
 * (Maven rule).
 */
public final class Exclusion {

    private final String group;
    private final String artifact;

    public Exclusion(final String group, final String artifact) {
        this.group = group;
        this.artifact = artifact;
    }

    /** Parses {@code "g:a"}; {@code *} allowed for either field. */
    public static Exclusion parse(final String s) {
        final String[] tokens = s.split(":", -1);
        if (tokens.length != 2) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                "Invalid exclusion (expected g:a, * allowed): " + s);
        }
        return new Exclusion(tokens[0], tokens[1]);
    }

    public boolean matches(final GA ga) {
        return ("*".equals(group) || group.equals(ga.group())) &&
            ("*".equals(artifact) || artifact.equals(ga.artifact()));
    }

    @Override
    public boolean equals(final Object o) {
        if (!(o instanceof Exclusion)) return false;
        final Exclusion other = (Exclusion) o;
        return group.equals(other.group) && artifact.equals(other.artifact);
    }

    @Override
    public int hashCode() {
        return group.hashCode() * 31 + artifact.hashCode();
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return group + ":" + artifact;
    }
}

/*
 * #%L
 * ImageJ software for multidimensional image processing and analysis.
 * %%
 * Copyright (C) 2009 - 2025 Board of Regents of the University of
 * Wisconsin-Madison, Broad Institute of MIT and Harvard, and Max Planck
 * Institute of Molecular Cell Biology and Genetics.
 * %%
 * Redistribution and use in source and binary forms, with or without
 * modification, are permitted provided that the following conditions are met:
 *
 * 1. Redistributions of source code must retain the above copyright notice,
 *    this list of conditions and the following disclaimer.
 * 2. Redistributions in binary form must reproduce the above copyright notice,
 *    this list of conditions and the following disclaimer in the documentation
 *    and/or other materials provided with the distribution.
 *
 * THIS SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED BY THE COPYRIGHT HOLDERS AND CONTRIBUTORS "AS IS"
 * AND ANY EXPRESS OR IMPLIED WARRANTIES, INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, THE
 * IMPLIED WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY AND FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE
 * ARE DISCLAIMED. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE COPYRIGHT HOLDERS OR CONTRIBUTORS BE
 * LIABLE FOR ANY DIRECT, INDIRECT, INCIDENTAL, SPECIAL, EXEMPLARY, OR
 * CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES (INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, PROCUREMENT OF
 * SUBSTITUTE GOODS OR SERVICES; LOSS OF USE, DATA, OR PROFITS; OR BUSINESS
 * INTERRUPTION) HOWEVER CAUSED AND ON ANY THEORY OF LIABILITY, WHETHER IN
 * CONTRACT, STRICT LIABILITY, OR TORT (INCLUDING NEGLIGENCE OR OTHERWISE)
 * ARISING IN ANY WAY OUT OF THE USE OF THIS SOFTWARE, EVEN IF ADVISED OF THE
 * POSSIBILITY OF SUCH DAMAGE.
 * #L%
 */

package net.imagej.updater.maven;

import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * A direct dependency edge: target identity at a concrete version, with
 * per-edge exclusions. Edges are publish-time facts (post model-building);
 * version ranges are forbidden by the index format.
 */
public final class DependencyEdge {

    private final GA ga;
    private final String version;
    private final List<Exclusion> exclusions;
    private final String platform;
    private final boolean optional;

    public DependencyEdge(final GA ga, final String version,
        final List<Exclusion> exclusions, final String platform,
        final boolean optional)
    {
        this.ga = ga;
        this.version = version;
        this.exclusions = exclusions == null ? //
            Collections.<Exclusion> emptyList() : //
            Collections.unmodifiableList(exclusions);
        this.platform = platform;
        this.optional = optional;
    }

    public GA ga() { return ga; }
    public String version() { return version; }
    public List<Exclusion> exclusions() { return exclusions; }
    public String platform() { return platform; }
    public boolean optional() { return optional; }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return ga + ":" + version;
    }
}

 * ARISING IN ANY WAY OUT OF THE USE OF THIS SOFTWARE, EVEN IF ADVISED OF THE
 * POSSIBILITY OF SUCH DAMAGE.
 * #L%

package net.imagej.updater.maven;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * One version of a component, with its direct dependency edges.
 * <p>
 * Slim entries carry identity+sha1 for local-file recognition only; they
 * have no (reliable) edges and are not installable.
 * </p>
 */
public final class Release {

    private final String version;
    private final List<DependencyEdge> edges = new ArrayList<>();
    private String sha1;
    private String timestamp;
    private long filesize = -1;
    private int minJava = -1;
    private String description;
    private boolean slim;

    public Release(final String version) {
        this.version = version;
    }

    public String version() { return version; }
    public List<DependencyEdge> edges() { return edges; }

    public String sha1() { return sha1; }
    public void setSha1(final String sha1) { this.sha1 = sha1; }

    public String timestamp() { return timestamp; }
    public void setTimestamp(final String timestamp) { this.timestamp = timestamp; }

    public long filesize() { return filesize; }
    public void setFilesize(final long filesize) { this.filesize = filesize; }

    public int minJava() { return minJava; }
    public void setMinJava(final int minJava) { this.minJava = minJava; }

    public String description() { return description; }
    public void setDescription(final String description) { this.description = description; }

    public boolean slim() { return slim; }
    public void setSlim(final boolean slim) { this.slim = slim; }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return version + (slim ? " (slim)" : "");
    }
}

/*

 * LIABLE FOR ANY DIRECT, INDIRECT, INCIDENTAL, SPECIAL, EXEMPLARY, OR

 * SUBSTITUTE GOODS OR SERVICES; LOSS OF USE, DATA, OR PROFITS; OR BUSINESS
 * INTERRUPTION) HOWEVER CAUSED AND ON ANY THEORY OF LIABILITY, WHETHER IN
 * CONTRACT, STRICT LIABILITY, OR TORT (INCLUDING NEGLIGENCE OR OTHERWISE)
 * ARISING IN ANY WAY OUT OF THE USE OF THIS SOFTWARE, EVEN IF ADVISED OF THE
 * POSSIBILITY OF SUCH DAMAGE.
 * #L%
 */

package net.imagej.updater.maven;

import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * All known versions of one {@link GA}, plus site-level flags.
 * <p>
 * Presence in a catalog is a <em>fact</em>, not an installation
 * instruction; only {@code offered} components can be enabled by users,
 * and the installed set is always the output of mediation.
 * </p>
 */
public final class MavenComponent {

    private final GA ga;
    private final Map<String, Release> releases = new LinkedHashMap<>();
    private boolean offered;
    private String current;

    public MavenComponent(final GA ga) {
        this.ga = ga;
    }

    public GA ga() { return ga; }
    public Map<String, Release> releases() { return releases; }

    public void add(final Release release) {
        releases.put(release.version(), release);
    }

    public Release release(final String version) {
        return releases.get(version);
    }

    public boolean offered() { return offered; }
    public void setOffered(final boolean offered) { this.offered = offered; }

    public String current() { return current; }
    public void setCurrent(final String current) { this.current = current; }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return ga.toString();
    }
}

 * INTERRUPTION) HOWEVER CAUSED AND ON ANY THEORY OF LIABILITY, WHETHER IN
 * CONTRACT, STRICT LIABILITY, OR TORT (INCLUDING NEGLIGENCE OR OTHERWISE)
 * ARISING IN ANY WAY OUT OF THE USE OF THIS SOFTWARE, EVEN IF ADVISED OF THE
 * POSSIBILITY OF SUCH DAMAGE.
 * #L%
 */

package net.imagej.updater.maven;

import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * One {@code <managed><version>} entry: a BOM-pinned version, optionally
 * with exclusions that apply to the managed component's outgoing edges
 * wherever it appears (Maven depMgmt-exclusion semantics).
 */
public final class ManagedEntry {

    private final GA ga;
    private final String version;
    private final List<Exclusion> exclusions;

    public ManagedEntry(final GA ga, final String version,
        final List<Exclusion> exclusions)
    {
        this.ga = ga;
        this.version = version;
        this.exclusions = exclusions == null ? //
            Collections.<Exclusion> emptyList() : //
            Collections.unmodifiableList(exclusions);
    }

    public GA ga() { return ga; }
    public String version() { return version; }
    public List<Exclusion> exclusions() { return exclusions; }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return ga + ":" + version;
    }
}

/*
 * #%L
 * ImageJ software for multidimensional image processing and analysis.
 * %%
 * Copyright (C) 2009 - 2025 Board of Regents of the University of
 * Wisconsin-Madison, Broad Institute of MIT and Harvard, and Max Planck
 * Institute of Molecular Cell Biology and Genetics.
 * %%
 * Redistribution and use in source and binary forms, with or without
 * modification, are permitted provided that the following conditions are met:
 *
 * 1. Redistributions of source code must retain the above copyright notice,
 *    this list of conditions and the following disclaimer.
 * 2. Redistributions in binary form must reproduce the above copyright notice,
 *    this list of conditions and the following disclaimer in the documentation
 *    and/or other materials provided with the distribution.
 *
 * THIS SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED BY THE COPYRIGHT HOLDERS AND CONTRIBUTORS "AS IS"
 * AND ANY EXPRESS OR IMPLIED WARRANTIES, INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, THE
 * IMPLIED WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY AND FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE
 * ARE DISCLAIMED. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE COPYRIGHT HOLDERS OR CONTRIBUTORS BE
 * LIABLE FOR ANY DIRECT, INDIRECT, INCIDENTAL, SPECIAL, EXEMPLARY, OR
 * CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES (INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, PROCUREMENT OF
 * SUBSTITUTE GOODS OR SERVICES; LOSS OF USE, DATA, OR PROFITS; OR BUSINESS
 * INTERRUPTION) HOWEVER CAUSED AND ON ANY THEORY OF LIABILITY, WHETHER IN
 * CONTRACT, STRICT LIABILITY, OR TORT (INCLUDING NEGLIGENCE OR OTHERWISE)
 * ARISING IN ANY WAY OUT OF THE USE OF THIS SOFTWARE, EVEN IF ADVISED OF THE
 * POSSIBILITY OF SUCH DAMAGE.
 * #L%
 */

package net.imagej.updater.maven;

import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * The union of component facts visible to mediation: all
 * {@code <component>} blocks (across all enabled sites) plus the
 * {@code <managed>} BOM entries.
 */
public final class ComponentCatalog {

    private final Map<GA, MavenComponent> components = new LinkedHashMap<>();
    private final Map<GA, ManagedEntry> managed = new LinkedHashMap<>();
    private String bom;

    public Map<GA, MavenComponent> components() { return components; }
    public Map<GA, ManagedEntry> managed() { return managed; }

    public String bom() { return bom; }
    public void setBom(final String bom) { this.bom = bom; }

    public MavenComponent component(final GA ga) {
        MavenComponent c = components.get(ga);
        if (c == null) {
            c = new MavenComponent(ga);
            components.put(ga, c);
        }
        return c;
    }

    public Release release(final GA ga, final String version) {
        final MavenComponent c = components.get(ga);
        return c == null ? null : c.release(version);
    }

    /** Merges another site's catalog into this one (union of facts). */
    public void merge(final ComponentCatalog other) {
        for (final MavenComponent theirs : other.components.values()) {
            final MavenComponent mine = component(theirs.ga());
            for (final Release release : theirs.releases().values()) {
                // Facts derive from immutable POMs; a full entry wins over a
                // slim entry, otherwise first writer wins.
                final Release existing = mine.release(release.version());
                if (existing == null || (existing.slim() && !release.slim())) {
                    mine.add(release);
                }
            }
            if (theirs.offered()) mine.setOffered(true);
            if (theirs.current() != null) mine.setCurrent(theirs.current());
        }
        for (final ManagedEntry entry : other.managed.values()) {
            if (!managed.containsKey(entry.ga())) managed.put(entry.ga(), entry);
        }
        if (bom == null) bom = other.bom;
    }
}

/*
 * #%L
 * ImageJ software for multidimensional image processing and analysis.
 * %%
 * Copyright (C) 2009 - 2025 Board of Regents of the University of
 * Wisconsin-Madison, Broad Institute of MIT and Harvard, and Max Planck
 * Institute of Molecular Cell Biology and Genetics.
 * %%
 * Redistribution and use in source and binary forms, with or without
 * modification, are permitted provided that the following conditions are met:
 *
 * 1. Redistributions of source code must retain the above copyright notice,
 *    this list of conditions and the following disclaimer.
 * 2. Redistributions in binary form must reproduce the above copyright notice,
 *    this list of conditions and the following disclaimer in the documentation
 *    and/or other materials provided with the distribution.
 *
 * THIS SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED BY THE COPYRIGHT HOLDERS AND CONTRIBUTORS "AS IS"
 * AND ANY EXPRESS OR IMPLIED WARRANTIES, INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, THE
 * IMPLIED WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY AND FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE
 * ARE DISCLAIMED. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE COPYRIGHT HOLDERS OR CONTRIBUTORS BE
 * LIABLE FOR ANY DIRECT, INDIRECT, INCIDENTAL, SPECIAL, EXEMPLARY, OR
 * CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES (INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, PROCUREMENT OF
 * SUBSTITUTE GOODS OR SERVICES; LOSS OF USE, DATA, OR PROFITS; OR BUSINESS
 * INTERRUPTION) HOWEVER CAUSED AND ON ANY THEORY OF LIABILITY, WHETHER IN
 * CONTRACT, STRICT LIABILITY, OR TORT (INCLUDING NEGLIGENCE OR OTHERWISE)
 * ARISING IN ANY WAY OUT OF THE USE OF THIS SOFTWARE, EVEN IF ADVISED OF THE
 * POSSIBILITY OF SUCH DAMAGE.
 * #L%
 */

package net.imagej.updater.maven;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Maven version ordering, per the version-order specification:
 * https://maven.apache.org/pom.html#version-order-specification
 * <p>
 * This is a port of jgo's implementation ({@code jgo.maven._version}),
 * which is the normative reference for this project; both must order the
 * shared corpus in {@code vectors/version-order.txt} identically.
 * </p>
 */
public final class MavenVersion implements Comparable<MavenVersion> {

    // Qualifier ordering per Maven spec:
    // alpha < beta < milestone < rc = cr < snapshot < "" = final = ga = release < sp
    private static final Map<String, Integer> QUALIFIER_ORDER = new HashMap<>();
    static {
        QUALIFIER_ORDER.put("alpha", -5);
        QUALIFIER_ORDER.put("a", -5);
        QUALIFIER_ORDER.put("beta", -4);
        QUALIFIER_ORDER.put("b", -4);
        QUALIFIER_ORDER.put("milestone", -3);
        QUALIFIER_ORDER.put("m", -3);
        QUALIFIER_ORDER.put("rc", -2);
        QUALIFIER_ORDER.put("cr", -2);
        QUALIFIER_ORDER.put("snapshot", -1);
        QUALIFIER_ORDER.put("", 0);
        QUALIFIER_ORDER.put("final", 0);
        QUALIFIER_ORDER.put("ga", 0);
        QUALIFIER_ORDER.put("release", 0);
        QUALIFIER_ORDER.put("sp", 1);
    }

    private static final class Token {
        final boolean numeric;
        final long num;
        final String str; // lowercased qualifier; only when !numeric
        final char sep; // '.', '-', '_', or 0 for the first token

        Token(final long num, final char sep) {
            this.numeric = true;
            this.num = num;
            this.str = null;
            this.sep = sep;
        }

        Token(final String str, final char sep) {
            this.numeric = false;
            this.num = 0;
            this.str = str;
            this.sep = sep;
        }

        boolean isNull() {
            return numeric ? num == 0 : //
                str.isEmpty() || "final".equals(str) || "ga".equals(str) ||
                "release".equals(str);
        }
    }

    private final String original;
    private final List<Token> tokens;

    public MavenVersion(final String version) {
        original = version;
        tokens = trimNulls(tokenize(version.trim()));
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return original;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean equals(final Object o) {
        return o instanceof MavenVersion && compareTo((MavenVersion) o) == 0;
    }

    @Override
    public int hashCode() {
        // Coarse but consistent with equals: equal versions trim to
        // token lists of equal length.
        return tokens.size();
    }

    public static int compare(final String v1, final String v2) {
        return new MavenVersion(v1).compareTo(new MavenVersion(v2));
    }

    @Override
    public int compareTo(final MavenVersion other) {
        final List<Token> a = tokens, b = other.tokens;
        final int maxLen = Math.max(a.size(), b.size());
        for (int i = 0; i < maxLen; i++) {
            final Token tokA = i < a.size() ? a.get(i) : null;
            final Token tokB = i < b.size() ? b.get(i) : null;
            // Padding separator comes from the corresponding position in
            // the longer version (per spec: pad with the shorter's "null"
            // of the other's separator kind).
            final char padSepA = i < b.size() ? b.get(i).sep : '.';
            final char padSepB = i < a.size() ? a.get(i).sep : '.';
            final int result = compareTokens(tokA, tokB, padSepA, padSepB);
            if (result != 0) return result;
        }
        return 0;
    }

    // -- Tokenization --

    private static List<Token> tokenize(final String version) {
        final List<Token> tokens = new ArrayList<>();
        if (version.isEmpty()) return tokens;

        final StringBuilder current = new StringBuilder();
        char currentSep = 0;
        int prevIsDigit = -1; // -1 unknown, 0 letter, 1 digit

        for (int i = 0; i < version.length(); i++) {
            final char c = version.charAt(i);
            if (c == '.' || c == '-' || c == '_') {
                if (current.length() > 0 || !tokens.isEmpty()) {
                    tokens.add(makeToken(current.toString(), currentSep));
                }
                current.setLength(0);
                currentSep = c;
                prevIsDigit = -1;
            }
            else if (Character.isDigit(c)) {
                if (prevIsDigit == 0 && current.length() > 0) {
                    tokens.add(makeToken(current.toString(), currentSep));
                    current.setLength(0);
                    currentSep = '-'; // digit/letter transitions act as hyphens
                }
                current.append(c);
                prevIsDigit = 1;
            }
            else {
                if (prevIsDigit == 1 && current.length() > 0) {
                    tokens.add(makeToken(current.toString(), currentSep));
                    current.setLength(0);
                    currentSep = '-';
                }
                current.append(c);
                prevIsDigit = 0;
            }
        }
        if (current.length() > 0 || !tokens.isEmpty()) {
            tokens.add(makeToken(current.toString(), currentSep));
        }
        return tokens;
    }

    private static Token makeToken(final String s, final char sep) {
        if (s.isEmpty()) return new Token(0L, sep);
        boolean numeric = true;
        for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
            if (!Character.isDigit(s.charAt(i))) {
                numeric = false;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (numeric) return new Token(Long.parseLong(s), sep);
        return new Token(s.toLowerCase(), sep);
    }

    private static List<Token> trimNulls(final List<Token> tokens) {
        final List<Token> result = new ArrayList<>(tokens);
        while (!result.isEmpty() && result.get(result.size() - 1).isNull()) {
            result.remove(result.size() - 1);
        }
        return result;
    }

    // -- Token comparison --

    private static int compareTokens(final Token a, final Token b,
        final char padSepA, final char padSepB)
    {
        if (a == null && b == null) return 0;

        // Effective values and separators; padding value is numeric 0 for
        // '.' separators and the empty qualifier otherwise.
        final boolean aNumeric = a != null ? a.numeric : padSepA == '.';
        final boolean bNumeric = b != null ? b.numeric : padSepB == '.';
        final long aNum = a != null ? a.num : 0;
        final long bNum = b != null ? b.num : 0;
        final String aStr = a != null ? a.str : "";
        final String bStr = b != null ? b.str : "";
        final char sepA = a != null ? a.sep : padSepA;
        final char sepB = b != null ? b.sep : padSepB;

        if (aNumeric && bNumeric) {
            // Separator dominates for numbers: -number < .number.
            if (sepA != sepB && isDelimiter(sepA) && isDelimiter(sepB)) {
                if (sepA == '.' && (sepB == '-' || sepB == '_')) return 1;
                if ((sepA == '-' || sepA == '_') && sepB == '.') return -1;
            }
            return Long.compare(aNum, bNum);
        }
        if (!aNumeric && !bNumeric) {
            final Integer knownA = QUALIFIER_ORDER.get(aStr);
            final Integer knownB = QUALIFIER_ORDER.get(bStr);
            final int prioA = knownA != null ? knownA : 0;
            final int prioB = knownB != null ? knownB : 0;
            if (prioA != prioB) return Integer.compare(prioA, prioB);
            // Same priority: known qualifiers compare as empty strings,
            // unknown ones alphabetically.
            final String cmpA = knownA != null ? "" : aStr;
            final String cmpB = knownB != null ? "" : bStr;
            return cmpA.compareTo(cmpB);
        }
        // Mixed: number > qualifier.
        return aNumeric ? 1 : -1;
    }

    private static boolean isDelimiter(final char c) {
        return c == '.' || c == '-' || c == '_';
    }
}
